## 0. Introduction ^0-0

Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa ^0-1
Abhidhammapiṭake ^0-2
Dhammasaṅgaṇīpāḷi ^0-3

## 1. Mātikā ^1-0

### 1.1 Tikamātikā ^1-1-0

(Ga) abyākatā dhammā. ^1-3

(Kha) sukhasahagatā dhammā. ^1-20

8. (Ka) dassanena pahātabbā dhammā. ^1-22
(Kha) bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā. ^1-23

9. (Ka) dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ^1-25

12. (Ka) parittā dhammā. ^1-34
(Kha) mahaggatā dhammā. ^1-35
(Ga) appamāṇā dhammā. ^1-36
13. (Ka) parittārammaṇā dhammā. ^1-37
(Kha) mahaggatārammaṇā dhammā. ^1-38

(Kha) majjhimā dhammā. ^1-41

20. (Ka) ajjhattā dhammā. ^1-58

124. (Ka) ajjavo ca. ^1-328
(Kha) maddavo ca. ^1-329
125. (Ka) khanti ca. ^1-330
(Kha) soraccañca. ^1-331
126. (Ka) sākhalyañca. ^1-332
(Kha) paṭisanthāro ca [paṭisandhāro ca (ka.)]. ^1-333
127. (Ka) indriyesu aguttadvāratā ca. ^1-334
(Kha) bhojane amattaññutā ca. ^1-335
128. (Ka) indriyesu guttadvāratā ca. ^1-336
(Kha) bhojane mattaññutā ca. ^1-337
129. (Ka) muṭṭhasaccañca. ^1-338
(Kha) asampajaññañca. ^1-339
130. (Ka) sati ca. ^1-340
(Kha) sampajaññañca. ^1-341
131. (Ka) paṭisaṅkhānabalañca. ^1-342
(Kha) bhāvanābalañca. ^1-343
132. (Ka) samatho ca. ^1-344
(Kha) vipassanā ca. ^1-345
133. (Ka) samathanimittañca . ^1-346
(Kha) paggāhanimittañca. ^1-347
134. (Ka) paggāho ca. ^1-348
(Kha) avikkhepo ca. ^1-349
135. (Ka) sīlavipatti ca. ^1-350
(Kha) diṭṭhivipatti ca. ^1-351
136. (Ka) sīlasampadā ca. ^1-352
(Kha) diṭṭhisampadā ca. ^1-353
137. (Ka) sīlavisuddhi ca. ^1-354
(Kha) diṭṭhivisuddhi ca. ^1-355
138. (Ka) diṭṭhivisuddhi kho pana. ^1-356
(Kha) yathādiṭṭhissa ca padhānaṃ. ^1-357
139. (Ka) saṃvego ca saṃvejaniyesu ṭhānesu. ^1-358
(Kha) saṃviggassa ca yoniso padhānaṃ. ^1-359
140. (Ka) asantuṭṭhitā ca kusalesu dhammesu. ^1-360
(Kha) appaṭivānitā ca padhānasmiṃ. ^1-361
141. (Ka) vijjā ca. ^1-362
(Kha) vimutti ca. ^1-363
142. (Ka) khaye ñāṇaṃ. ^1-364
(Kha) anuppāde ñāṇanti. ^1-365
Suttantikadukamātikā [suttantamātikā (syā.)]. ^1-366
Mātikā niṭṭhitā. ^1-367

## 2. Cittuppādakaṇḍaṃ ^2-0

### 2.1 Kāmāvacarakusalaṃ ^2-1-0

### 2.2 Padabhājanī ^2-2-0

1. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ [viriyindriyaṃ (sī. syā.)] hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ [viriyabalaṃ (sī. syā.)] hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, paññābalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, amoho hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi [kāyappassaddhi (syā.)] hoti, cittapassaddhi [cittappassaddhi (syā.)] hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti, kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā [kāyujjukatā (sī. ka.)] hoti, cittujukatā [cittujjukatā (sī. ka.)] hoti, sati hoti, sampajaññaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, vipassanā hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-1
2. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^2-2
3. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti. ^2-3
4. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti. ^2-4
5. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ [sañcetayitattaṃ (syā.)] – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti. ^2-5
6. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti. ^2-6
7. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti. ^2-7
8. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhānatā anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti. ^2-8
9. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti. ^2-9
10. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti. ^2-10
11. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti. ^2-11
12. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-12
13. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho [viriyārambho (sī. syā.)] nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī [ussoḷhi (sī.)] thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-13
14. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-14
15. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-15
16. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-16
17. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-17
18. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-18
19. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tesaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā [irīyanā (sī.)] vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-19
20. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-20
21. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti. ^2-21
22. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti. ^2-22
23. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti. ^2-23
24. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti. ^2-24
25. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti. ^2-25
26. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti. ^2-26
27. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti. ^2-27
28. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti. ^2-28
29. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti. ^2-29
30. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti. ^2-30
31. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti. ^2-31
32. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti. ^2-32
33. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ [adūsanā adūsitattaṃ (syā.)] abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? ^2-33
34. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi amoho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti. ^2-34
35. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye anabhijjhā hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anabhijjhā hoti. ^2-35
36. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye abyāpādo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye abyāpādo hoti. ^2-36
37. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-37
38. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye hirī hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirī hoti. ^2-38
39. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappaṃ hoti. ^2-39
40. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyapassaddhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi [paṭippassaddhi (sī. syā.)] passambhanā paṭipassambhanā [paṭippassambhanā (sī. syā.)] paṭipassambhitattaṃ [paṭippassambhitattaṃ (sī. syā.)] – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyapassaddhi hoti. ^2-40
41. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittapassaddhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittapassaddhi hoti. ^2-41
42. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyalahutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyalahutā hoti. ^2-42
43. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittalahutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittalahutā hoti. ^2-43
44. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyamudutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyamudutā hoti. ^2-44
45. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittamudutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittamudutā hoti. ^2-45
46. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyakammaññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa kammaññatā kammaññattaṃ kammaññabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyakammaññatā hoti. ^2-46
47. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittakammaññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa kammaññatā kammaññattaṃ kammaññabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittakammaññatā hoti. ^2-47
48. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyapāguññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyapāguññatā hoti. ^2-48
49. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittapāguññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittapāguññatā hoti. ^2-49
50. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyujukatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyujukatā hoti. ^2-50
51. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittujukatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittujukatā hoti. ^2-51
52. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti. ^2-52
53. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sampajaññaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sampajaññaṃ hoti. ^2-53
54. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti. ^2-54
55. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vipassanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vipassanā hoti. ^2-55
56. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti. ^2-56
57. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti. ^2-57
Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-58
Padabhājanīyaṃ. ^2-59
Paṭhamabhāṇavāro. ^2-60
### 2.3 Koṭṭhāsavāro ^2-3-0

58. Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti, ekā vedanā hoti, ekā saññā hoti, ekā cetanā hoti, ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti, eko vedanākkhandho hoti, eko saññākkhandho hoti, eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti, eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti, ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti, ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti, ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti, ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-61
59. Katame tasmiṃ samaye cattāro khandhā honti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho. ^2-62
60. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti. ^2-63
61. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti. ^2-64
62. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā [thapetvā (ka.)] vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti. ^2-65
63. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti ? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti. ^2-66
Ime tasmiṃ samaye cattāro khandhā honti. ^2-67
64. Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye dvāyatanāni honti? Manāyatanaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ. ^2-68
65. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti. ^2-69
66. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti. ^2-70
Imāni tasmiṃ samaye dvāyatanāni honti. ^2-71
67. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dve dhātuyo honti? Manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. ^2-72
68. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti. ^2-73
69. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti. ^2-74
Imā tasmiṃ samaye dve dhātuyo honti. ^2-75
70. Katame tasmiṃ samaye tayo āhārā honti? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro , viññāṇāhāro. ^2-76
71. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phassāhāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phassāhāro hoti. ^2-77
72. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye manosañcetanāhāro hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manosañcetanāhāro hoti. ^2-78
73. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇāhāro hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇāhāro hoti. ^2-79
Ime tasmiṃ samaye tayo āhārā honti. ^2-80
74. Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye aṭṭhindriyāni honti? Saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ. ^2-81
75. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-82
76. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-83
77. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-84
78. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-85
79. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-86
80. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-87
81. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-88
82. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tesaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti . ^2-89
Imāni tasmiṃ samaye aṭṭhindriyāni honti. ^2-90
83. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti? Vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. ^2-91
84. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti. ^2-92
85. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhānatā anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti. ^2-93
86. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti. ^2-94
87. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti. ^2-95
88. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti. ^2-96
Idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti. ^2-97
89. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti? Sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^2-98
90. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-99
91. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti. ^2-100
92. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti. ^2-101
93. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti. ^2-102
94. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti. ^2-103
Ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti. ^2-104
95. Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye satta balāni honti? Saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, paññābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, ottappabalaṃ. ^2-105
96. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti. ^2-106
97. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti. ^2-107
98. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti. ^2-108
99. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti. ^2-109
100. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti. ^2-110
101. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti. ^2-111
102. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti. ^2-112
Imāni tasmiṃ samaye satta balāni honti. ^2-113
103. Katame tasmiṃ samaye tayo hetū honti? Alobho, adoso, amoho. ^2-114
104. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti. ^2-115
105. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti. ^2-116
106. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti. ^2-117
Ime tasmiṃ samaye tayo hetū honti. ^2-118
107. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko phasso hoti. ^2-119
108. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā vedanā hoti. ^2-120
109. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā saññā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā saññā hoti. ^2-121
110. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā cetanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā cetanā hoti. ^2-122
111. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti. ^2-123
112. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko vedanākkhandho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko vedanākkhandho hoti. ^2-124
113. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko saññākkhandho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko saññākkhandho hoti. ^2-125
114. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti. ^2-126
115. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti. ^2-127
116. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti. ^2-128
117. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-129
118. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti. ^2-130
119. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho , saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti. ^2-131
120. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā dhammadhātu hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā dhammadhātu hoti. ^2-132
Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-133
Koṭṭhāsavāro. ^2-134
### 2.4 Suññatavāro ^2-4-0

121. Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye dhammā honti, khandhā honti, āyatanāni honti, dhātuyo honti, āhārā honti, indriyāni honti, jhānaṃ hoti, maggo hoti, balāni honti, hetū honti, phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vedanākkhandho hoti, saññākkhandho hoti, saṅkhārakkhandho hoti, viññāṇakkhandho hoti, manāyatanaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, manoviññāṇadhātu hoti, dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-135
122. Katame tasmiṃ samaye dhammā honti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime tasmiṃ samaye dhammā honti. ^2-136
123. Katame tasmiṃ samaye khandhā honti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho , saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime tasmiṃ samaye khandhā honti. ^2-137
124. Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye āyatanāni honti? Manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samaye āyatanāni honti. ^2-138
125. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dhātuyo honti? Manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā tasmiṃ samaye dhātuyo honti. ^2-139
126. Katame tasmiṃ samaye āhārā honti? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – ime tasmiṃ samaye āhārā honti. ^2-140
127. Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye indriyāni honti? Saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samaye indriyāni honti. ^2-141
128. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jhānaṃ hoti? Vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jhānaṃ hoti. ^2-142
129. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye maggo hoti? Sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye maggo hoti. ^2-143
130. Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye balāni honti? Saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, paññābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, ottappabalaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samaye balāni honti. ^2-144
131. Katame tasmiṃ samaye hetū honti? Alobho, adoso, amoho – ime tasmiṃ samaye hetū honti. ^2-145
132. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^2-146
133. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti. ^2-147
134. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti. ^2-148
135. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti? ^2-149
136. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti…pe… idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti. ^2-150
137. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti. ^2-151
138. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti. ^2-152
139. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti. ^2-153
140. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti. ^2-154
141. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti…pe… idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti. ^2-155
142. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti…pe… idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-156
143. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti. ^2-157
144. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti. ^2-158
145. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti. ^2-159
Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-160
Suññatavāro. ^2-161
Paṭhamaṃ cittaṃ. ^2-162
146. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-163
Dutiyaṃ cittaṃ. ^2-164
147. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti , manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi hoti, cittapassaddhi hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti, kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā hoti, cittujukatā hoti, sati hoti, samatho hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^2-165
Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, sattindriyāni honti , pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti, cha balāni honti, dve hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^2-166
148. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso anabhijjhā abyāpādo hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati samatho paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-167
Tatiyaṃ cittaṃ. ^2-168
149. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-169
Catutthaṃ cittaṃ. ^2-170
150. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, upekkhā hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, upekkhindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, paññābalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, amoho hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi hoti, cittapassaddhi hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā hoti, cittujukatā hoti, sati hoti, sampajaññaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, vipassanā hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-171
151. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^2-172
152. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti…pe…. ^2-173
153. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye upekkhā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye upekkhā hoti…pe…. ^2-174
154. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye upekkhindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye upekkhindriyaṃ hoti…pe… ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^2-175
Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti , dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^2-176
155. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo. ^2-177
Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-178
Pañcamaṃ cittaṃ. ^2-179

Yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ākāsānañcāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati upekkhāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-322
266. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma viññāṇañcāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati upekkhāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-323

268. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasaññāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati upekkhāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-325

Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… chandādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīriyādhipateyyaṃ…pe… cittādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīmaṃsādhipateyyaṃ…pe… chandādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… vīriyādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… cittādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… vīmaṃsādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-328

272. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… chandādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīriyādhipateyyaṃ…pe… cittādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīmaṃsādhipateyyaṃ…pe… chandādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… vīriyādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… cittādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ…pe… vīmaṃsādhipateyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pe… majjhimaṃ…pe… paṇītaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-332

### 2.31 Lokuttarakusalaṃ ^2-31-0

### 2.32 Suddhikapaṭipadā ^2-32-0

277. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvācā hoti, sammākammanto hoti, sammāājīvo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, paññābalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, amoho hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi hoti, cittapassaddhi hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti, kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā hoti, cittujukatā hoti, sati hoti, sampajaññaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, vipassanā hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-339
278. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^2-340
279. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti. ^2-341
280. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti. ^2-342
281. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti. ^2-343
282. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti. ^2-344
283. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti. ^2-345
284. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhānatā anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti. ^2-346
285. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa pītisambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti. ^2-347
286. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti. ^2-348
287. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti. ^2-349
288. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-350
289. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-351
290. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-352
291. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-353
292. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-354
293. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-355
294. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-356
295. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tesaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-357
296. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ anaññātānaṃ adiṭṭhānaṃ appattānaṃ aviditānaṃ asacchikatānaṃ sacchikiriyāya paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-358
297. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-359
298. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti. ^2-360
299. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammāvācā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye catūhi vacīduccaritehi ārati virati paṭivirati veramaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti velāanatikkamo setughāto sammāvācā maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāvācā hoti. ^2-361
300. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammākammanto hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tīhi kāyaduccaritehi ārati virati paṭivirati veramaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti velāanatikkamo setughāto sammākammanto maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammākammanto hoti. ^2-362
301. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāājīvo hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye micchāājīvā ārati virati paṭivirati veramaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti velāanatikkamo setughāto sammāājīvo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāājīvo hoti. ^2-363
302. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti. ^2-364
303. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti. ^2-365
304. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti. ^2-366
305. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti. ^2-367
306. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti. ^2-368
307. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti. ^2-369
308. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti. ^2-370
309. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti. ^2-371
310. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti. ^2-372
311. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti. ^2-373
312. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti. ^2-374
313. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti. ^2-375
314. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti. ^2-376
315. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye anabhijjhā hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anabhijjhā hoti. ^2-377
316. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye abyāpādo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye abyāpādo hoti. ^2-378
317. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-379
318. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye hirī hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirī hoti. ^2-380
319. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappaṃ hoti. ^2-381
320. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyapassaddhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ passaddhisambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyapassaddhi hoti. ^2-382
321. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittapassaddhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ passaddhisambojjhaṅgo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittapassaddhi hoti. ^2-383
322. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyalahutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyalahutā hoti. ^2-384
323. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittalahutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittalahutā hoti. ^2-385
324. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyamudutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyamudutā hoti. ^2-386
325. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittamudutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittamudutā hoti. ^2-387
326. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyakammaññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa kammaññatā kammaññattaṃ kammaññabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyakammaññatā hoti. ^2-388
327. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittakammaññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa kammaññatā kammaññattaṃ kammaññabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittakammaññatā hoti. ^2-389
328. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyapāguññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyapāguññatā hoti. ^2-390
329. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittapāguññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittapāguññatā hoti. ^2-391
330. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyujukatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyujukatā hoti. ^2-392
331. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittujukatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittujukatā hoti. ^2-393
332. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti. ^2-394
333. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sampajaññaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sampajaññaṃ hoti. ^2-395
334. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti. ^2-396
335. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vipassanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vipassanā hoti. ^2-397
336. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo vīriyasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti. ^2-398
337. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-399
Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, navindriyāni honti, pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti, ekā vedanā hoti, ekā saññā hoti, ekā cetanā hoti, ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti, eko vedanākkhandho hoti, eko saññākkhandho hoti, eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti, eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti, ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti, ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti, ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti, ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^2-400
338. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvācā sammākammanto sammāājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-401
339. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-402
340. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-403
341. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-404
342. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā …pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ…pe… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ…pe… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ…pe… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-405
Suddhikapaṭipadā. ^2-406
### 2.33 Suññataṃ ^2-33-0

343. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-407
344. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-408
Suññataṃ. ^2-409
### 2.34 Suññatamūlakapaṭipadā ^2-34-0

345. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-410
346. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-411
347. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ suññataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-412
348. Katame dhammā kusalā? ### 2.37 Vīsati mahānayā ^2-37-0

357. Katame dhammā kusalā? ### 2.38 Adhipati ^2-38-0

358. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ chandādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīriyādhipateyyaṃ…pe… cittādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīmaṃsādhipateyyaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-427
359. Katame dhammā kusalā? 360. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ maggaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ indriyaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ balaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ bojjhaṅgaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ saccaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ samathaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ dhammaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ khandhaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ āyatanaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ dhātuṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ āhāraṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ phassaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ vedanaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ saññaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ cetanaṃ bhāveti…pe… lokuttaraṃ cittaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ chandādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīriyādhipateyyaṃ…pe… cittādhipateyyaṃ…pe… vīmaṃsādhipateyyaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-429

361. Katame dhammā kusalā? Dutiyo maggo. ^2-433
362. Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye lokuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāveti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ anavasesappahānāya tatiyāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ , tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… aññindriyaṃ hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-434
Tatiyo maggo. ^2-435
363. Katame dhammā kusalā? 364. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye aññindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tesaṃ dhammānaṃ ñātānaṃ diṭṭhānaṃ pattānaṃ viditānaṃ sacchikatānaṃ sacchikiriyāya paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpannaṃ, idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye aññindriyaṃ hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^2-437
Catuttho maggo. ^2-438

### 2.39 Dvādasa akusalāni ^2-39-0

365. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, micchādiṭṭhi hoti, micchāsaṅkappo hoti, micchāvāyāmo hoti, micchāsamādhi hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, ahirikabalaṃ hoti, anottappabalaṃ hoti, lobho hoti, moho hoti, abhijjhā hoti, micchādiṭṭhi hoti, ahirikaṃ hoti , anottappaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti ; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā akusalā. ^2-440
366. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^2-441
367. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti. ^2-442
368. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti. ^2-443
369. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti. ^2-444
370. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti. ^2-445
371. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā micchāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti. ^2-446
372. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhānatā anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti. ^2-447
373. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti. ^2-448
374. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti. ^2-449
375. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti. ^2-450
376. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-451
377. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? ^2-452
378. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-453
379. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-454
380. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tesaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti. ^2-455
381. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye micchādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantāro diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ gāho patiṭṭhāho [paṭiggāho (sī. syā.)] abhiniveso parāmāso kummaggo micchāpatho micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāho [vipariyesagāho (ka.)] – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye micchādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-456
382. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye micchāsaṅkappo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā micchāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye micchāsaṅkappo hoti. ^2-457
383. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye micchāvāyāmo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye micchāvāyāmo hoti. ^2-458
384. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye micchāsamādhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye micchāsamādhi hoti. ^2-459
385. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti. ^2-460
386. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti. ^2-461
387. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ahirikabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye na hirīyati hiriyitabbena na hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ahirikabalaṃ hoti. ^2-462
388. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anottappabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye na ottappati ottappitabbena na ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anottappabalaṃ hoti. ^2-463
389. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye lobho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye lobho lubbhanā lubbhitattaṃ sārāgo sārajjanā sārajjitattaṃ abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye lobho hoti. ^2-464
390. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye moho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye aññāṇaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayo ananubodho asambodho appaṭivedho asaṃgāhanā apariyogāhanā asamapekkhanā apaccavekkhanā apaccakkhakammaṃ dummejjhaṃ bālyaṃ asampajaññaṃ moho pamoho sammoho avijjā avijjogho avijjāyogo avijjānusayo avijjāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ avijjālaṅgī moho akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye moho hoti. ^2-465
391. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye abhijjhā hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye lobho lubbhanā lubbhitattaṃ sārāgo sārajjanā sārajjitattaṃ abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye abhijjhā hoti. ^2-466
392. Katamā tasmiṃ samaye micchādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantāro diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ gāho patiṭṭhāho abhiniveso parāmāso kummaggo micchāpatho micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye micchādiṭṭhi hoti. ^2-467
393. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ahirikaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye na hirīyati hiriyitabbena na hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ahirikaṃ hoti. ^2-468
394. Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anottappaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye na ottappati ottappitabbena na ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anottappaṃ hoti. ^2-469
395. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti. ^2-470
396. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti. ^2-471
397. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā akusalā. ^2-472
Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, pañcindriyāni honti, pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti, cattāri balāni honti, dve hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā akusalā…pe…. ^2-473
398. Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappo micchāvāyāmo micchāsamādhi vīriyabalaṃ samādhibalaṃ ahirikabalaṃ anottappabalaṃ lobho moho abhijjhā micchādiṭṭhi ahirikaṃ anottappaṃ samatho paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā akusalā. ^2-474
399. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā akusalā. ^2-475
400. Katame dhammā akusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, micchāsaṅkappo hoti, micchāvāyāmo hoti, micchāsamādhi hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, ahirikabalaṃ hoti, anottappabalaṃ hoti, lobho hoti, moho hoti, abhijjhā hoti, ahirikaṃ hoti, anottappaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā akusalā…pe…. ^2-476
